```sql
SELECT
    u."ID",
    u."UUID",
    u."FIRSTNAME",
    u."LASTNAME",
    u."EMAIL",
    u."ACCOUNT_UUID",
    u."STATUS",
    u."CREATED_AT",
    u."IS_BLOCKED",
    a."USER_UUID",
    a."AUTH0_USER_ID",
    a."CREATED_AT" AS auth0_created_at
FROM
    "USERS" u
LEFT JOIN
    "AUTH0_USERS" a
    ON u."UUID" = a."USER_UUID"
LEFT JOIN
    "AUTH0_ORGS" o
    ON u."ACCOUNT_UUID" = o."ACCOUNT_UUID"
WHERE
    u."EMAIL" IN ('misiuwmfolo@tiscali.it',
'shoxha@searchlightcap.com',
'thomasmeyerpay@mail.ru',
'tigipa@thichanthit.com');

```

Me and [[Avantika]] while updating [[Adena]] we found out that Lifecycle Rule for S3 bucket is different from November until Today. 

The code change that I introduced is not anymore enabled from November.


Before November the code was correct but somehow changed.

Code Snippet from c4: ╭─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╮
│ src/modules/saas/aws-sdk-v1/aws.go                                  │
│─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────│
│ config_s3 := &s3.BucketLifecycleConfiguration{                      │
│   Rules: []*s3.LifecycleRule{                                       │
│     {                                                               │
│       ID:     aws.String("Delete expired backups"),                 │
│       Filter: &s3.LifecycleRuleFilter{Prefix: aws.String("")},      │
│       Status: aws.String("Enabled"),                                │
│       NoncurrentVersionExpiration: &s3.NoncurrentVersionExpiration{ │
│         NoncurrentDays: aws.Int64(7),                               │
│       },                                                            │
│       Expiration: &s3.LifecycleExpiration{                          │
│         ExpiredObjectDeleteMarker: aws.Bool(true),                  │
│       },                                                            │
│     },                                                              │
│   },                                                                │
│ }                                                                   │
- [ ] ╰─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯

## Weekly Brief Notes - Week 15 (2025-04-07 to 2025-04-11)

### Key Highlights:
1. **Database for Thomas Bleisfleisch**: Continued work on the database for Thomas Bleisfleisch ensuring it matches the version used in the Adidas POC.
2. **AWS Billing Alert Fix**: Worked on correcting the titles of billing alerts for every AWS account.
3. **Resource Allocation Issue with Adena**:
   - Adena encountered insufficient capacity issues with the r5d.8xlarge instance type. The team worked with AWS Support and managed to secure needed instances through persistence.
   - A strategy call was held with Mihai, Andrew, Juergen, and me to explore quick solutions for maintaining Adena’s database functionality during resource constraints.
4. **SaaS Retrospective**: Participated in the SaaS retrospective meeting to analyze ongoing progress and challenges.
5. **General Work Follow-up**: Addressed issues flagged during retrospective sessions.

---

- SaaS Retrospective meeting.

- Adena has hit insufficient capacity with instance type r5d.8xlarge; had a call with AWS Support and kept trying until we got instances needed.
- Me [[Mihai]], [[Andrew]], and [[Juergen]] had a call about Adena and potential solutions for their database.

## week15.md
```
### Weekly Notes: Week 15 (April 7 - April 13, 2025)

**Key Updates:**

1. **Database Work:**
   - Continued work on creating a database for [[Thomas Bleistfleich]] with specifications matching the Adidas POC version.
   - Addressed instance capacity issues for Adena, particularly with type r5d.8xlarge. AWS Support suggested repeated attempts to secure the required instances, which ultimately worked.

2. **Meetings & Collaboration:**
   - SaaS Retrospective meeting held. Action items included fixing the titles of billing alerts for all AWS accounts.

## quarter_review_new.md
```
To create a detailed self-review note for the quarter, including tasks and customer involvement with an additional column for specific implementations like VPC peering and DirectConnect, here’s a consolidated report based on the provided notes:

---

### **Self-Review Notes for Q2 2025**

#### **Tasks Overview**

| Date       | Task/Activity Description                                                                                           | Customers Involved               | Implementation Details (e.g., VPC Peering, Direct Connect, etc.)                   |
|------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|----------------------------------|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| 2025-06-30 | Added two new IPs to routing table. Fixed database issue in VENI due to job full exception.                        | DeutschlandCard, VENI            | Routing table IP updates. Database troubleshooting for VENI.                      |
| 2025-06-20 | Analytics database downtime troubleshooting. Collaborated on Formue issue resolution.                              | Formue                           | General database troubleshooting.                                                 |
| 2025-04-30 | Established Direct Connect between GCP and Equinix Metal.                                                          | Equinix Metal                    | Successfully configured Direct Connect.                                            |
| 2025-02-11 | Alerts improvement discussion, secret update for Datadog, created SPOT ticket for availability monitor issue.       | Adidas POC                       | Improved monitoring and warnings workflows.                                        |
| 2025-02-04 | Developed a script to update Datadog monitor settings.                                                             | Datadog                          | Script implementation for monitor adjustments.                                     |
| 2025-06-04 | Addressed production issue (missing c4 image) and fixed image repository configuration.                            | Internal Production Environment  | Configured c4 images in the repository.                                           |
| 2025-02-25 | Led cleanup of logs for L3-2778 ticket. Fixed deployment issues.                                                   | Leila                            | Log collection and policy updates.                                                |
| 2025-04-24 | Enabled EventBridge rule in ct account to trigger SaaS analytics pipeline.                                          | Mihai, Avantika                  | EventBridge configuration in production SaaS pipeline.                           |
| 2025-04-10 | Updated bucket access script based on internal suggestions. Resolved shared memory issue in staging with Avantika. | Adena                           | Bucket access policy change. Staging system stabilization via shared memory fixes. |

#### **Collaboration and Meetings**
- Participated in SaaS-Retrospective and Planning Meetings involving DeutschlandCard (2025-06-30).
- Regular refinement and sprint planning discussions on project tickets, including SPOT Jira tickets.
- Collaborated with Gerardo, Swen, Nico, Andrew during critical outage troubleshooting for Formue databases.
- Supported multiple PR reviews for Vahid, Haibo, and Avantika, contributing to repository quality and development processes.

---

#### **Customer-Specific Contributions**
Customers such as Formue, VENI, Adidas POC, Equinix, DeutschlandCard, among others, were supported with technical implementation tasks. 

| Customer           | Contribution                                                                                              | Details                                                                                           |
|--------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| DeutschlandCard    | Added two routing table IPs (2025-06-30).                                                                | Network-specific routing updates.                                                              |
| VENI               | Resolved database synchronization issue (2025-06-30).                                                   | Complex database fixes bypassing monitoring limitations.                                       |
| Formue             | Addressed database issues and downtime prevention (e.g., 2025-06-20).                                    | Provided L3 support and debugging.                                                             |
| Equinix Metal      | Implemented Direct Connect setup successfully between Equinix and GCP (2025-04-30).                      | Significant network infrastructure configuration task.                                         |
| Adidas POC         | Educated customer on account deactivation implications and reactivation timelines (2025-02-11).          | Process advocacy and improvements in data retention policies.                                  |

---

#### **Implementation and Technical Highlights**
- **Direct Connect Configuration**: Key achievement was successfully creating a Direct Connect link between GCP and Equinix Metal, ensuring stable and efficient connectivity.
- **Database Fixes**: Significant debugging and resolution of database connection issues, including synchronization challenges, for customers like VENI and Formue.
- **Routing Table Management**: Enabled network routing changes for DeutschlandCard customer.
- **Image Repository Management**: Fixed repository settings for c4 images in production environments.
- **Script Development**: Created scripts to automate and streamline monitoring settings and log policy changes.

---

### **Reflection and Recommendations**
#### **Strengths**
- Hands-on troubleshooting and timely resolution of complex database and network-level issues.
- Strong collaborative efforts across teams and external customers.
- Notable achievements in infrastructure implementations like Direct Connect and routing updates.

#### **Areas for Improvement**
- More proactive monitoring strategies to avoid escalation of issues (e.g., untriggered VENI database alerts).
- Enhanced documentation for custom scripts and configurations to reduce debugging time.

#### **Plans for Next Quarter**
- Expand infrastructure automation tasks to streamline future implementations (e.g., VPC peering, monitoring).
- Contribute actively to project refinement and planning meetings, addressing pending tickets.
- Foster better communication between customers and release teams for clarity on versioning and compatibility fixes.

---

This detailed note should help in presenting your contributions effectively during the self-review session, while highlighting key achievements and areas for improvement.
```

## weeki.md
```
Here’s a compiled weekly note based on the daily notes for May 6 to May 9, 2025:

---

## Weekly Notes: May 5 - May 9, 2025

### Overview
This week was focused on development and collaboration efforts surrounding SaaS-related projects, pull request reviews, and the progression of key tickets like PrivateLink for Adidas. Below is a summary of tasks and progress from the week.

---

### Daily Highlights

#### **May 6, 2025**
- Continued work on **PrivateLink for Adidas** ([SPOT-24891](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-24891)).
- Pair programming session with [[Avantika]] on debugging issues related to **data pipeline optimizations**.
- Attended a knowledge-sharing meeting on **load balancing best practices** for SaaS platforms.
- Drafted technical documentation for the new SaaS feature rollout.

---

#### **May 7, 2025**
- Reviewed and approved [[Mihai]]'s pull request for **feature flags implementation** ([SPOT-24785](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-24785)).
- Made progress on the PrivateLink implementation:
  - Completed initial setup for **security group policies**.
  - Coordinated with Adidas's cloud team to align integration requirements.
- Provided feedback on the **error logging feature** to [[Avantika]] ([SPOT-24859](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-24859)).
- Internal discussions focused on **scaling database connections** for new customers.

---

#### **May 8, 2025**
- Finalized **PrivateLink network configuration** ([SPOT-24891](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-24891)) and documented setup processes for future customers.
- Troubleshot and resolved a bug in the API warehouse integration layer.
- Collaborated with the UX team on the design review for the **Admin Dashboard Enhancements** project.
- Began drafting a tutorial on configuring **PrivateLink setups** as part of the SaaS documentation update.

---

#### **May 9, 2025**
- Submitted the initial version of the PrivateLink setup to Adidas for testing.
- Attended a bi-weekly retrospective meeting; discussed:
  - Successes: **PrivateLink project progress, effective pairing sessions.**
  - Challenges: Time constraints on the new **load management automation feature**.
- Assigned action items for the next sprint:
  - [[Mihai]]: Finalize feature flags rollout.
  - [[Avantika]]: Address pending PR feedback for error logging improvements.
- Closed the week with a brainstorming session on automated **monitoring and metrics collection** for future SaaS integrations.

---

### Key Accomplishments
- Significant progress on the **PrivateLink for Adidas** project ([SPOT-24891](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-24891)).
- Successfully reviewed and supported PRs from team members ([[Avantika]] and [[Mihai]]).
- Enhanced collaboration through pair programming and internal knowledge-sharing sessions.
- Contributed toward SaaS scalability and documentation improvements.

---

### Next Steps
- Test and iterate based on Adidas's feedback for the PrivateLink setup.
- Plan for completing the **load management automation feature** rollout.
- Schedule follow-up meetings with stakeholders for the next phase of SaaS platform improvements.

--- 

Let me know if you'd like further adjustments or more details added!
```

## week.md
```
Here’s a compiled **Weekly Notes** based on the content provided from April 25 to April 30:

---

### **Weekly Notes: April 24 – April 30, 2025**

#### **Key Topics**
- Collaboration and discussions around AWS EventBridge Rules and StepFunction usage.
- Maintenance and release updates for the SaaS analytics pipeline and monitoring of system SLI.
- Significant teamwork involving [[Avantika]], [[Mihai]], and [[Haibo]] to address pipeline dispatching challenges.

---

#### **Highlights from Daily Notes**

### **April 25, 2025**
- Continued work on implementing an EventBridge rule in the CT account targeting the production account to trigger the SaaS analytics pipeline.
- Updated SLI metrics and discussed strategies for monitoring thresholds during maintenance windows.
- Call with [[Mihai]] to explore StepFunction architecture enhancements.

---

### **April 28, 2025**
- Maintenance window in production concluded successfully. SaaS release version: `20250417`.
- Conducted post-release analysis to ensure full functionality of SaaS analytics workflows were restored and optimized.
- Troubleshooting minor discrepancies in StepFunction state transitions during dispatch processes with [[Haibo]].

---

### **April 29, 2025**
- Focus shifted to debugging cross-account routing errors in EventBridge configurations.
- Collaborated with [[Avantika]] and [[Mihai]] to refine the integration testing framework for better validation of inter-account triggers.
- Finalized a checklist for system-wide monitoring using enhanced SLI metrics and StepFunction introspection.

---

### **April 30, 2025**
- Wrapped up week-long efforts on the EventBridge-Production pipeline. Integration is now streamlined.
- Retroactive review of maintenance events highlighted significant improvements in system telemetry.
- Brief meeting with [[Avantika]] and [[Haibo]] to document best practices for managing inter-service dispatch mechanisms.

---

#### **Action Items for Next Week**
1. **EventBridge Rules**: Establish protocols for automated retries in case of cross-account errors.
2. **SLI Metrics**: Address edge-case monitoring gaps identified during post-maintenance review.
3. **StepFunction Optimization**: Review execution visualization and document bottleneck mitigation processes.

---

### **Team Mentions**
Collaboration played a vital role in resolving challenges and optimizing solutions this week:
- [[Avantika]]: Played a critical role in EventBridge setup and integration testing.
- [[Mihai]]: Contributed valuable insights to enhance StepFunction architecture and execution flow.
- [[Haibo]]: Assisted in fine-tuning dispatch error resolution processes.

--- 

By consolidating individual contributions and focusing on systematic improvements, excellent progress was made in managing critical workflows throughout this week. Looking forward to building on these learnings in the upcoming collaboration sprint.
```

id: "2024-08-19"
aliases:
  - August 19, 2024
tags:
  - daily-notes
---

# August 19, 2024

Checking monitoring with Mihai.
PostActions tries to send 'set_main_cluster_state' (which is missing)
The CloudFormation update attempt failed because it involved a non-standard operation: updating, deleting, or getting an SSM parameter. According to Irakli, the issue stems from the initial setup where the database was running on c4 version 4.13, which doesn't support CloudFormation template updates. The database was subsequently upgraded to @saas-8.28.4 using an older method (stopping all nodes), and that update was successful. However, c4 attempted to update the CloudFormation template today for reasons that are not known, resulting in a failure due to missing policies.
```

I talked with Gabriel, and he told me that moving to the new database will include everything, even passwords. So, we can't use the new database's password even if it's shown; we need to stick with the old password.

We ran the command to get the list of SCHEMA TABLES that are corrupted, in this case EXA_STATISTICS with the list of tables.

The next step based on the documentation is to follow and create DDL for a Table. We ran the script create_table_ddl.sql to get the DDL.

and after that we ran the command:

execute script exa_toolbox.create_table_ddl('SOURCE_SCHEMA', 'SOURCE_TABLE', 'TARGET_SCHEMA', 'TARGET_TABLE', true) ;

SQL_EXA> execute script exa_toolbox.create_table_ddl('EXA_STATISTICS', '$EXA_STATS_OPTIMIZATION', 'EXA_STATISTICS', '$EXA_STATS_OPTIMIZATION', true);
EXA: execute script exa_toolbox.create_table_ddl('EXA_STATISTICS', '$EXA_ST...

CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE "EXA_STATISTICS"."$EXA_STATS_OPTIMIZATION"(     "SCHEMA_NAME" VARCHAR(128) UTF8,      "TABLE_NAME" VARCHAR(128) UTF8,      "TABLE_ID" DECIMAL(18,0),      "OBJECT_ID" DECIMAL(18
,0),      "OWNER" VARCHAR(128) UTF8,      "REMARKS" VARCHAR(100000) UTF8,      "START_TIME" TIMESTAMP(3),      "STOP_TIME" TIMESTAMP(3),      "OPERATION_ID" DECIMAL(12,0),      "SUCCESS" CHAR(1) UTF8,
      "USER_NAME" VARCHAR(128) UTF8 ); COMMENT ON TABLE "EXA_STATISTICS"."$EXA_STATS_OPTIMIZATION" is '';

dwad_client snapshot-restore $(dwad_client shortlist) 4 Rqxxwn-uTomsZackmkialw/id_9889/node_3/snapshot_backup_202410081100

How can we tell if the data is up-to-date?

To check data integrity in the database, use this command:

```
checkdata -pers_volume_id 0 -mode data_integrity
```

To make sure all checksums are correct, use:

```
checkdata -pers_volume_id 0 -mode checksum
```

We found corrupted data in the old database after running these checks and looking at the logs, there are a large number of data blocks that are corrupt.

Questions:

1. Why is the old database still working without any connection issues, even though there's corruption?
2. How can we check if any data is missing?

Why Phantom blocks happened in new database?
Is it because of a sync between the old and new database, is corrupting data on the way?
```

tags:

---

# July 7, 2025

Checking prod-saas-offline account on why the cost is high. The reason is Request Tier 1, meaning S3 bucket request.

[[Andrew]] asked to preserve logs and copy of the bucket, for further investigation of VENI database:kLiEKd0KSBu5jfwxzYM78w

[[Mihai]] and me trying to understand why cost uploaded from cost explorer are not matching with costs from cost explorer UI. We come to conclusion that the data we get from cost explorer for saas costs is hourly based, but for saas analytics we decided to change on daily basis, meaning the data of the previous day is uploaded in next day of the previous day. We updated the configuration to upload data hourly.
```

# May 2, 2024

Time: 08:01 AM

Pushed three changes in review section

https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-20498
https://github.com/exasol/saas-terraform/pull/147
https://github.com/exasol/saas-terraform/pull/182

Time: 8:30 AM

Adena complaining that API call to get the costs is not working.

```
curl -H "Authorization: Bearer <pat-token>" https://cloud.exasol.com/api/v1/accounts/<org-id>/usage?yearMonth=2023-12&type=csv

<pat-token>: You'll have to use a personal access token, monitoring read provides sufficient permissions
<org-id>: Is your account ID beginning with org_
```

Time: 08:51 AM

Worked on the L3 ticket: https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/L3-1846

Time: 09:34 AM

// TODO:

```
Increase thread limit on all nodes:
cat /proc/sys/kernel/threads-max
echo "750000" >/proc/sys/kernel/threads-max
```

Permanent Change:

vim /etc/sysctl.d/99-custom.conf

```
threads-max = 750000
```

Time: 12:30 - 15:23
Outage:

Wooga DB couldn't started because of the insufficient aws capacity.

After 25mins or more finally we were able to get back the instances.

But the next problem was that c4 update was corrupted, and we didn't want to restart the instances again (we might loose them again.)
Therefore we used a new c4 command to update without restarting the instances.
command:

```
CCC_UPDATE_SPACE_NEEDED=15 c4 update cluster -t @exasol-8.26.1 -P -f
```

links: [[Wooga]]
```

## 2024-06-14.md
```
---
id: "2024-06-14"
aliases:
  - June 14, 2024
tags:
  - daily-notes
---

# June 14, 2024

09:42 AM

Checking the costs history for a ticket : https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-20928

10:41 AM

Creating a list of onboarding new members. [onboarding-newmember](daily_notes/how-to/onboarding-newmember.md)

11:14 AM

Test 8.28.2 release in staging database with new c4 update cluster command.

Semirkon c4 version: 0.4.10
it is not possible to follow with the new c4 update cluster command, as it does not support it.

Env: staging
DBUUID: k0I5h3ITQFae8UZcxUokiQ
region: eu-central-1

Command used to update specific release version:

```bash
CONFD_TOKEN=exa_pat_XXXXXXX confd_client saas_db_create_custom_size -a '{"db_name": "SemikronUpdateTest", "platform_name": "aws", "region": "eu-central-1", "size": "XS1", "account_uuid": "org_9aWFmH2ys3gEGhwb", "auto_stop_enabled": True, "auto_stop_idle_time": 120, "version": "@exasol-8.18.2"}'
```

update failed to 8.28.2 the reason is that c4 version doesn't support

since the c4 version is old, we have to update it:

```bash
ccc_wc=$(c4 info @exasol-8.28.2 Dependencies.CCC)
c4 fetch "$ccc_wc"
sudo systemctl stop c4_cloud_command c4
sudo cp ~/.ccc$(c4 info "$ccc_wc" Local-Linkdir)/bin/c4 $(which c4)
sudo systemctl start c4_cloud_command c4 --no-block

```

and then run the command without updating the template:

CCC_UPDATE_TEMPLATE=false c4 update cluster -f -t @exasol-8.28.2 -p <play-id>
```

# October 9, 2024

I talked with Gabriel, and he told me that moving to the new database will include everything, even passwords. So, we can't use the new database's password even if it's shown; we need to stick with the old password.

We ran the command to get the list of SCHEMA TABLES that are corrupted, in this case EXA_STATISTICS with the list of tables.

The next step based on the documentation is to follow and create DDL for a Table. We ran the script create_table_ddl.sql to get the DDL.

and after that we ran the command:

execute script exa_toolbox.create_table_ddl('SOURCE_SCHEMA', 'SOURCE_TABLE', 'TARGET_SCHEMA', 'TARGET_TABLE', true) ;

SQL_EXA> execute script exa_toolbox.create_table_ddl('EXA_STATISTICS', '$EXA_STATS_OPTIMIZATION', 'EXA_STATISTICS', '$EXA_STATS_OPTIMIZATION', true);
EXA: execute script exa_toolbox.create_table_ddl('EXA_STATISTICS', '$EXA_ST...

## DDL

CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE "EXA_STATISTICS"."$EXA_STATS_OPTIMIZATION"(     "SCHEMA_NAME" VARCHAR(128) UTF8,      "TABLE_NAME" VARCHAR(128) UTF8,      "TABLE_ID" DECIMAL(18,0),      "OBJECT_ID" DECIMAL(18
,0),      "OWNER" VARCHAR(128) UTF8,      "REMARKS" VARCHAR(100000) UTF8,      "START_TIME" TIMESTAMP(3),      "STOP_TIME" TIMESTAMP(3),      "OPERATION_ID" DECIMAL(12,0),      "SUCCESS" CHAR(1) UTF8,
      "USER_NAME" VARCHAR(128) UTF8 ); COMMENT ON TABLE "EXA_STATISTICS"."$EXA_STATS_OPTIMIZATION" is '';

1 row in resultset.

SQL_EXA> DROP TABLE EXA_STATISTICS."$EXA_STATS_OPTIMIZATION"
1    > ;
EXA: DROP TABLE EXA_STATISTICS."$EXA_STATS_OPTIMIZATION"...
Error: [40005] Successfully reconnected after internal server error, transaction was rolled back. (SessionID: 1812423672814698496)

Dropping table failing.

## 2024-07-03.md
```
---
id: "2024-07-03"
aliases:
  - July 3, 2024
tags:
  - daily-notes
---

# July 3, 2024

```bash

list of commands:

// list the databases per account (use the command from management saas)
confd_client saas_db_list account_uuid: <account_uuid>

// list clusters per account (use the command from management saas)
confd_client saas_cluste_list account_uuid: <account_uuid> db_uuud: <db_uuid>

// stop database (use the command from management saas)
confd_client saas_db_stop account_uuid: <account_uuid> db_uuud: <db_uuid>

//(list services that cos manages)

# cosps -n

// execute command in all nodes

# cosexec -r -a <execution command>

// execute command in all nodes option

# psh <execution command>

// kill service

# cosrm -a <pid>

// confd_client commands (inside the db)

dwad_client list
confd_client infra_db_stop db_name: <db_name>
confd_client infra_db_start db_name: <db_name>
dwad_client print-setup <db_name>
```

## quarter_review.md
```
### Consolidated Detailed Notes for Quarterly Self-Review

Here’s a comprehensive summary of the activities and tasks based on the provided notes:

---

#### **Technical Implementations and Troubleshooting**
1. **VPC Peering and DirectConnect Implementation:**
   - Successfully achieved Direct Connection between **Google Cloud Platform (GCP)** and **Equinix Metal** ([SPOT-24811](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-24811)).

2. **Database Operations:**
   - Investigated issues with databases across various environments such as production, staging, and development:
     - **Formue** database (multiple troubleshooting tickets handled, including resizing issues and c4 version updates).
     - **Adena**, **Semikron**, and **Vocare** system patches updated successfully to version `8.28.2`.
     - Resolving database connectivity issues (e.g., JDBC driver errors).
   - Handled corrupted data and schema optimization (`EXA_STATISTICS -> $EXA_STATS_OPTIMIZATION` issue).
   - Fixed production databases suffering from **phantom blocks** during sync operations.

3. **Linux Image Updates:**
   - Conducted kernel module check and update for AWS EC2 systems (`e1000e`).

4. **Monitoring & Analytics:**
   - Updated **SLI Dashboards** and monitoring scripts for databases.
   - Created and upgraded **Datadog Monitors**:
     - Updated settings like “Require Full Window” and alarm thresholds.
     - Troubleshot production-level issues with Datadog API keys and service authorizer exceptions.
   - Addressed **cost anomalies** for clusters and provided analysis to management ([SPOT-25824](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-25824)).

5. **Cloud Operations & Security:**
   - Configured **AWS CloudTrail** and ensured proper tagging (`SAAS-1673` activated cost allocation tags).
   - Cleanup tasks for **cancelled accounts** and orphaned resources like:
     - CloudWatch log groups ([SPOT-21765](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-21765)).
     - Aging accounts (resize warnings for inactive accounts within 14 days).

6. **EventBridge Automation:**
   - Created **EventBridge rules** in CT accounts targeting production to trigger pipelines.

---

#### **Collaboration and Team Efforts**
1. **Team Collaboration:**
   - Worked alongside several team members on critical issues:
     - Escalations resolved with Avantika, Mihai, Haibo, Andrew, and Gerardo on multiple occasions.
     - Onboarding training provided to new members (e.g., Avantika).

2. **Customer-Specific Tasks:**
   - Direct support and collaboration with customers:
     - **Formue**, **AdidasPOC**, **Vocare**, **BlueYonder**, **Wooga** debugging and patch updates.
     - Billing-related discussions with **Equinix**.

3. **Peer Development:**
   - Reviewed multiple GitHub PRs from team members (e.g., Vahid, Haibo, Mihai) and provided feedback:
     - Introduced Git processes (`ruff --diff` flag for better troubleshooting).

4. **Security Meetings:**
   - Participated in security champion meetings and enhanced incident-response workflows (`extension-bucket-user access/key rotation discussions`).

---

#### **Enhancements and Strategic Initiatives**
1. **Automation and Scripts:**
   - Created:
     - **Datadog monitoring scripts**.
     - **Log management** enhancements based on **team suggestions** ([SPOT-24668](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-24668)).

2. **Code Contributions and Improvements:**
   - Contributed to **SaaS Terraform** with feature upgrades and fixes:
     - Publishing sensors, improving database recovery events ([SPOT-25385](https://github.com/exasol/saas-terraform)).
     - Added better filtering/conditions for monitoring configurations ([PR#267](https://github.com/exasol/saas-terraform/pull/267)).
   - Addressed CI failing issues for SaaS API build pipelines.

3. **Cost Optimizations:**
   - Investigated spikes in **production account costs**.
   - Provided updates regarding **AWS quota service utilization** and **S3 non-current version cleanup**.

4. **Platform Stability:**
   - Fixed SaaSD failures and conducted a systematic investigation of root causes impacting uptime.

---

#### **Personal Development**
1. **Training & Learning Sessions:**
   - Completed Compliance Training on LinkedIn Learning.

2. **Language Skills:**
   - Included language improvement efforts (**Deutsch Lernen**).

---

#### **Tickets Handled**
1. **Feature Requests and Bug Fixes:**
   - Created/Completed multiple Jira tickets ranging from SaaS improvements ([SPOT-20897](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-20897), SPOT-21022, SPOT-23846) to customer escalations ([L3-2072](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/L3-2072), L3-3177, L3-3144).

2. **Code Review Feedback**
   - Provided constructive reviews for PRs across teams (e.g., SaaS Terraform, API services).

---

### Additional Column: **Customer Involvement Summary**

| **Customer**        | **Task/Context**                                                                                                                                         |
|----------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| **Formue**          | Fixed release issues, c4 updates, restarting database nodes in production, troubleshooting session (ticket [L3-1985](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/L3-1985)). |
| **BlueYonder**      | Cluster update and monitoring ([SPOT-25962](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-25962)).                                                             |
| **DeutschlandCard** | Networking adjustments (routing table update).                                                                                                           |
| **AdidasPOC**       | Account deactivation workflow resolved ([L3-3148](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/L3-3148)).                                                         |
| **Wooga**           | Lifecyc policy configured to delete old databases and buckets.                                                                                           |
| **Semikron/Vocare** | Updated SaaS clusters to the latest version `8.28.2`.                                                                                                    |
| **Equinix**         | Achieved Direct Connect between GCP and Equinix ([SPOT-24811](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-24811)).                                          |

---

This consolidated document serves as an overview for your self-review. It highlights your impactful contributions across technical implementations, collaboration, customer engagement, and personal growth initiatives during the quarter.

## group_by_week.sh
```
#!/bin/bash

# Make sure this script is run with Bash, not sh
if [ -z "$BASH_VERSION" ]; then
  echo "Please run this script with Bash: bash $0"
  exit 1
fi

TARGET_DIR="${1:-.}"
OUTPUT_DIR="${2:-./weekly_output}"

mkdir -p "$OUTPUT_DIR"

# Clear previous temp list if exists
TMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)

# Step 1: Collect files and group by week
find "$TARGET_DIR" -maxdepth 1 -type f -print0 | while IFS= read -r -d '' file; do
  # Get modification time
  mod_epoch=$(stat -f "%m" "$file")
  week=$(date -r "$mod_epoch" +%G-W%V)

  # Append file path to the week-based temp file
  echo "$file" >> "$TMP_DIR/$week.list"
done

# Step 2: Create markdown files
for week_file in "$TMP_DIR"/*.list; do
  week=$(basename "$week_file" .list)
  output_file="$OUTPUT_DIR/$week.md"
  echo "# Files for Week $week" > "$output_file"
  echo "" >> "$output_file"

  while IFS= read -r file; do
    echo "## $(basename "$file")" >> "$output_file"
    echo '```' >> "$output_file"
    cat "$file" >> "$output_file"
    echo '```' >> "$output_file"
    echo "" >> "$output_file"
  done < "$week_file"

  echo "Created: $output_file"
done

# Clean up
rm -r "$TMP_DIR"

```

[[Unattended-upgrades]]

Create a custom database in test account.
Will follow the changes, to run update 15mins after the boot of the system.
[Unattended-upgrades.md]

```bash
apt-daily.timer to update the package lists (apt-get update), and
apt-daily-upgrade.timer to install the upgrades (unattended-upgrade).

Summary of Configurations
/etc/systemd/system/apt-daily.timer.d/override.conf:


This configuration ensures apt-daily.timer will trigger 15 minutes.

/etc/systemd/system/apt-daily-upgrade.timer.d/override.conf:


[Unit]
Description=Run daily repository update

[Timer]
OnCalendar=
OnBootSec=15min
OnUnitActiveSec=1d
AccuracySec=1h
RandomizedDelaySec=30min
Persistent=true

[Install]
WantedBy=timers.target

This configuration ensures apt-daily-upgrade.timer will trigger 35 minutes

[Unit]
Description=Run daily package upgrade
After=apt-daily.timer

[Timer]
OnCalendar=
OnUnitActiveSec=1d
AccuracySec=1h
RandomizedDelaySec = 30 min
Persistent=true

[Install]
WantedBy=timers.target

uncommneted the following lines in the file: /etc/apt/apt.conf.d/50unattended-upgrades

"${distro_id}:${distro_codename}-updates";
```

Create a bucket as requested: https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-20763

## 2024-11-21.md

id: "2024-11-21"
aliases:

  - daily-notes
---

# November 21, 2024

Collect sql query output and added to L3 Wooga, and assigned Gabriel Moruz

Refinement of the https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-22188, we need to improve exalogrotate

```bash
if we have to collect logs for current date, the exa-log-collect need to trigger exalogrotate for current date first, wait for exalogrotate to finish (we need to catch the exit code), then collect logs for current date.

Haibo will give some hints on how to trigger exalogrotate.

exalogrotate is trigger every day (for previous day) and logs are uploaded to s3.

logs are uploaded to s3 prefix to each node prefix (n10,n11,n12 etc).

logs are compressed and uploaded to s3.

if the range is requested more than 1 day, the exa-log-collect need to be modified to collect logs from multiple days.

Note: disabled rule to apply unattended-upgrade to production database, because we found out that unattended-upgrade updated docker.service which is dependency of ecs for DLHC and for that reason c4_cloud_command.service was also restarted.

## 2025-06-22.md
```
---
id: "2025-06-22"
aliases:
  - June 22, 2025
tags:
  - daily-notes
---

# June 22, 2025

Found out that unattended-upgrades restarts c4_cloud_command on new Ubuntu 24.04 LTS. We had to add a conf to not restart the service.

```
/etc/needrestart/conf.d/exasol.conf

# Do not automatically restart c4_cloud_command
# because that's the service which runs the whole
# Exasol stack of services, including databases.
$nrconf{override_rc}{qr(^c4_cloud_command\.service$)} = 0;

```
```

On call with [[Mihai]] [[Andrew]] [[Fabian]] about users that got hacked and we had to check whether these users are still in the SaaS.

```sql
SELECT
    u."ID",
    u."UUID",
    u."FIRSTNAME",
    u."LASTNAME",
    u."EMAIL",
    u."ACCOUNT_UUID",
    u."STATUS",
    u."CREATED_AT",
    u."IS_BLOCKED",
    a."USER_UUID",
    a."AUTH0_USER_ID",
    a."CREATED_AT" AS auth0_created_at
FROM
    "USERS" u
LEFT JOIN
    "AUTH0_USERS" a
    ON u."UUID" = a."USER_UUID"
LEFT JOIN
    "AUTH0_ORGS" o
    ON u."ACCOUNT_UUID" = o."ACCOUNT_UUID"
WHERE
    u."EMAIL" IN ('misiuwmfolo@tiscali.it',
'shoxha@searchlightcap.com',
'thomasmeyerpay@mail.ru',
'tigipa@thichanthit.com');

```
```

# March 17, 2025


Reviewed [[Haibo]] ticket for testing database with 1 node and 4 nodes https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-24210

Reviewing [[Avantika]] PR for removing DBConfigure function and using StepFunction instead. SPOT-24272

Reviewing the suggestion for my PR's

Discussion with [[Vahid]] about the ticket: https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-24326


We have [[Adena]] update today to a version : @saas-8.29.8


Me and [[Avantika]] while updating [[Adena]] we found out that Lifecycle Rule for S3 bucket is different from November until Today. 

The code change that I introduced is not anymore enabled from November.


Before November the code was correct but somehow changed.

Code Snippet from c4: ╭─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╮
│ src/modules/saas/aws-sdk-v1/aws.go                                  │
│─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────│
│ config_s3 := &s3.BucketLifecycleConfiguration{                      │
│   Rules: []*s3.LifecycleRule{                                       │
│     {                                                               │
│       ID:     aws.String("Delete expired backups"),                 │
│       Filter: &s3.LifecycleRuleFilter{Prefix: aws.String("")},      │
│       Status: aws.String("Enabled"),                                │
│       NoncurrentVersionExpiration: &s3.NoncurrentVersionExpiration{ │
│         NoncurrentDays: aws.Int64(7),                               │
│       },                                                            │
│       Expiration: &s3.LifecycleExpiration{                          │
│         ExpiredObjectDeleteMarker: aws.Bool(true),                  │
│       },                                                            │
│     },                                                              │
│   },                                                                │
│ }                                                                   │
- [ ] ╰─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯
```

### Weekly Notes: March 31 - April 4, 2025

- **DLHC Meeting**: April 4

## DeutschlandCard-meeting.md
```
---
id: DeutschlandCard-meeting
aliases: []
tags:
  - DeutschlandCard
---

### **Step-by-Step Guide: AWS Direct Connect Setup**

#### **1. Plan Your Setup**
   - **Determine Your Needs**: Decide what you need the Direct Connect connection for (e.g., connecting AWS to Oracle Cloud (OCI) through FastConnect).
   - **Location**: Identify the AWS Direct Connect **location** (data center) that is geographically closest to your hardware or OCI region.
   - **Connection Speed**: Choose a connection capacity (e.g., 1 Gbps or 10 Gbps).
   - **Existing vs New Setup**:
     - If you already have an existing Direct Connect, you can reuse it, as mentioned.
     - If you're starting fresh, you'll need to provision a new Direct Connect.

---

#### **2. Request AWS Direct Connect**
   - Log in to your **AWS Management Console**.
   - Go to the **Direct Connect dashboard**.
   - Select "Create a New Connection" and complete the following details:
     - **Location**: Choose an AWS Direct Connect location.
     - **Port speed**: Specify your desired capacity (e.g., 1 Gbps or 10 Gbps).
     - **Connection Type**: Options include a dedicated network connection (physical) or a hosted connection (via an AWS Direct Connect Partner).
   - After submitting the request, AWS Direct Connect will allocate a port or virtual interface (VIF).

---

#### **3. Order a Physical Cross-Connect**
   - Work with either:
     - **AWS Direct Connect Partners** or your colocation provider to set up the physical cross-connect to the AWS Direct Connect router at the selected location/data center.
   - AWS provides a Letter of Authority (LOA)–Cable Diagram after approval, which is required to complete the setup of the connection at the colocation facility.

---

#### **4. Configure the Virtual Interface (VIF)**
   - After the physical cross-connect is established, log in to the **AWS Management Console** to create a **Virtual Interface (VIF)**.
   - There are two types of VIFs to choose from:
     1. **Private VIF**: For securely accessing private resources like VPCs.
     2. **Public VIF**: For accessing AWS public services (e.g., S3, DynamoDB).
   - For OCI integration, you’ll likely configure a **Private VIF** to connect your VPC to the Oracle Cloud.

---

#### **5. Set Up AWS Side: Configure VPC Settings**
   - Link the Direct Connect to your AWS **Virtual Private Gateway (VGW)** or **Transit Gateway**:
     - Create a **Virtual Private Gateway** in AWS if you don’t already have one.
     - Attach this gateway to the VPC hosting your EXASOL SaaS service.
   - This facilitates the connection between the Direct Connect VIF and your resources.

---

#### **6. Set Up Oracle Cloud Side: Configure OCI FastConnect**
   - Work within your Oracle Cloud Infrastructure (OCI) console:
     - Use OCI **FastConnect** to establish a connection between your Oracle networks and AWS Direct Connect.
     - Define routing policies between your Oracle Virtual Cloud Network (VCN) and AWS Virtual Private Gateway.
   - Provision a **FastConnect Virtual Circuit**, ensuring it aligns with the AWS Direct Connect setup.

---

#### **7. Configure BGP Peering**
   - Direct Connect uses **Border Gateway Protocol (BGP)** for routing. Configure BGP both on:
     - AWS side: Specify your ASN (Autonomous System Number) and other BGP settings.
     - OCI side: Set up BGP peering with the corresponding route information.
   - Exchange routes between your AWS VPC and OCI VCN.

---

#### **8. Test Connectivity**
   - Verify that traffic flows between your AWS VPC hosting EXASOL and your Oracle Cloud account through the VPN tunnel and Direct Connect.
   - Test for latency, throughput, and security expectations.

---

#### **9. Monitor and Adjust**
   - Use AWS tools such as **CloudWatch** for monitoring the connection.
   - If needed, adjust the speed of Direct Connect monthly (1 Gbps to higher speeds).
   - Ensure no other database access occurs outside of the VPN tunnel, as EXASOL would handle this restriction.

---

### Costs and Customer Responsibility
- The customer is responsible for all AWS Direct Connect and OCI FastConnect costs. This includes colocation charges, connection speed adjustments, and any outbound traffic costs.

---

```

## week.md
```
Here’s a compiled **Weekly Notes** based on the content provided from April 25 to April 30:

---

### **Weekly Notes: April 24 – April 30, 2025**

#### **Key Topics**
- Collaboration and discussions around AWS EventBridge Rules and StepFunction usage.
- Maintenance and release updates for the SaaS analytics pipeline and monitoring of system SLI.
- Significant teamwork involving [[Avantika]], [[Mihai]], and [[Haibo]] to address pipeline dispatching challenges.

---

#### **Highlights from Daily Notes**

### **April 25, 2025**
- Continued work on implementing an EventBridge rule in the CT account targeting the production account to trigger the SaaS analytics pipeline.
- Updated SLI metrics and discussed strategies for monitoring thresholds during maintenance windows.
- Call with [[Mihai]] to explore StepFunction architecture enhancements.

---

### **April 28, 2025**
- Maintenance window in production concluded successfully. SaaS release version: `20250417`.
- Conducted post-release analysis to ensure full functionality of SaaS analytics workflows were restored and optimized.
- Troubleshooting minor discrepancies in StepFunction state transitions during dispatch processes with [[Haibo]].

---

### **April 29, 2025**
- Focus shifted to debugging cross-account routing errors in EventBridge configurations.
- Collaborated with [[Avantika]] and [[Mihai]] to refine the integration testing framework for better validation of inter-account triggers.
- Finalized a checklist for system-wide monitoring using enhanced SLI metrics and StepFunction introspection.

---

### **April 30, 2025**
- Wrapped up week-long efforts on the EventBridge-Production pipeline. Integration is now streamlined.
- Retroactive review of maintenance events highlighted significant improvements in system telemetry.
- Brief meeting with [[Avantika]] and [[Haibo]] to document best practices for managing inter-service dispatch mechanisms.

---

#### **Action Items for Next Week**
1. **EventBridge Rules**: Establish protocols for automated retries in case of cross-account errors.
2. **SLI Metrics**: Address edge-case monitoring gaps identified during post-maintenance review.
3. **StepFunction Optimization**: Review execution visualization and document bottleneck mitigation processes.

---

### **Team Mentions**
Collaboration played a vital role in resolving challenges and optimizing solutions this week:
- [[Avantika]]: Played a critical role in EventBridge setup and integration testing.
- [[Mihai]]: Contributed valuable insights to enhance StepFunction architecture and execution flow.
- [[Haibo]]: Assisted in fine-tuning dispatch error resolution processes.

--- 

By consolidating individual contributions and focusing on systematic improvements, excellent progress was made in managing critical workflows throughout this week. Looking forward to building on these learnings in the upcoming collaboration sprint.

## Weekly Notes: May 5 - May 9, 2025

- Schedule follow-up meetings with stakeholders for the next phase of SaaS platform improvements.

---

## 2025-06-22.md
```
---
id: "2025-06-22"
aliases:
  - June 22, 2025
tags:
  - daily-notes
---

# June 22, 2025

Found out that unattended-upgrades restarts c4_cloud_command on new Ubuntu 24.04 LTS. We had to add a conf to not restart the service.

```
/etc/needrestart/conf.d/exasol.conf

# Do not automatically restart c4_cloud_command
# because that's the service which runs the whole
# Exasol stack of services, including databases.
$nrconf{override_rc}{qr(^c4_cloud_command\.service$)} = 0;

```
```

## touch
```
# Weekly Report (Week 26)

## Overview
This week was focused on debugging production issues, addressing cost concerns in SaaS accounts, reviewing and creating pull requests, troubleshooting customer-specific issues, and fixing infrastructure-related challenges with new Ubuntu 24.04 configurations.

---

### Key Activities

#### Production Issues and Cost Investigation
- **June 20**
  - Analytics database and other databases in production were down due to issues identified in release `8.29.10`.
  - Collaborated with [[Gerardo]], [[Swen]], [[Nico]], and [[Andrew]] on [[Formue]] production issues. Resolved the immediate problem, and [[Andrew]] handled follow-up calls with the customer.
  - Investigated and replied to [[Juergen]] on cost spikes in production accounts (Ticket: [SPOT-25824](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-25824)).
  - Worked on L3 tickets:
    - [[L3-3144]]: Debugged and analyzed critical customer issues.
    - [[AdidasNeptun]] resizing issue ([L3-3148](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/L3-3148)).

- **June 23**
  - Resolved [[DeutschlandCard]] scaling issues by updating the c4 image ([L3-3151](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/L3-3151)).
  - Addressed issues with reserved capacity leading to cost problems. Created a ticket to implement alerts for reserved capacity ([SPOT-25890](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-25890)).
  - On discussion with [[Formue]] regarding Ubuntu 24.04 LTS.

- **June 25**
  - Cost analysis in `prod-saas-customer` and `prod-saas-offline` accounts:
    - [[Formue]] increased S3 bucket data usage in the `Offline` account.
    - Identified unused capacity reservation for `r5d.8xlarge` in the `prod-saas-customer` account for [[Adena]].
  - Created an investigative ticket ([SPOT-25890](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-25890)) to address these cost inefficiencies as prioritized by [[Juergen]].

---

#### Pull Request (PR) Reviews and Code Fixes
- **June 24:** Reviewed PRs for:
  - [[Vahid]]: [PR vz.SPOT-25385](https://github.com/exasol/saas-terraform/pull/vz.SPOT-25385).
  - [[Haibo]]:
    - [PR #527](https://github.com/exasol/saas-terraform/pull/527).
    - [PR #529](https://github.com/exasol/saas-terraform/pull/529).
  - [[Vahid]]: [PR #530](https://github.com/exasol/saas-terraform/pull/530).

- **June 26:** Reviewed PRs for:
  - [[Haibo]]: [PR #533](https://github.com/exasol/saas-terraform/pull/533).
  - [[Leila]]: [PR #537](https://github.com/exasol/saas-terraform/pull/537).

---

#### Terraform and Infrastructure Updates
- **June 25:**
  - Fixed Terraform provider version inconsistencies across workflows and modules.
  - Resolved old [[localstack]] version issues.

- **June 22 & June 26:**
  - Found and implemented solutions for Ubuntu `24.04` LTS `unattended-upgrades` restarting `c4_cloud_command.service`. 
    - Added a configuration to prevent automatic restarts:
      ```
      /etc/needrestart/conf.d/exasol.conf

      # Do not automatically restart c4_cloud_command
      $nrconf{override_rc}{qr(^c4_cloud_command\.service$)} = 0;
      ```
    - Deployed this fix, but encountered database errors due to service restarts caused by unattended upgrades.

---

#### Customer-Specific Issues
- **DeutschlandCard:**
  - **June 23:** Fixed scaling issue by updating c4 image ([L3-3151](https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/L3-3151)).
  - **June 26:** Troubleshot LDAP network issues related to external IP NATing.

- **Formue:**
  - **June 20:** Collaborated on production issue resolution with customer.
  - **June 23:** Investigated the scaling issue caused by excessive data input.

- **Other Customers:**
  - Supported [[Alex]] to merge PRs and unblock the DLHC workflow.
  - Worked on troubleshooting [[L3-3144]] and related tickets.

---

### Collaboration and Escalations
- Worked closely with [[Vahid]], [[Haibo]], [[Leila]], [[Alex]], [[Gerardo]], [[Juergen]], and others on PR reviews, debugging, and cost investigations.
- Escalated cost-related inefficiencies to [[Juergen]] for prioritization.
- Collaborated with [[DeutschlandCard]], [[Formue]], and others on production and infrastructure troubleshooting.

---

### Challenges and Follow-ups
- Addressed Terraform provider inconsistencies and localstack version compatibility issues.

# June 22, 2025

Found out that unattended-upgrades restarts c4_cloud_command on new Ubuntu 24.04 LTS. We had to add a conf to not restart the service.

```
/etc/needrestart/conf.d/exasol.conf

# Do not automatically restart c4_cloud_command
# because that's the service which runs the whole
# Exasol stack of services, including databases.
$nrconf{override_rc}{qr(^c4_cloud_command\.service$)} = 0;

```

## Week_26.md
```
**Weekly Report: June 19, 2025 - June 26, 2025**

### Summary of Activities:

This week involved multiple efforts across L3 tickets, troubleshooting customer issues, cost optimization initiatives, PR reviews, and infrastructure fixes. Here's a breakdown of key actions and accomplishments:

---

### **Customer Support and Troubleshooting:**  
1. **Formue Issues:**
   - June 19: Attempted fixes for persistent production issues.
   - June 20: Resolved production database outage after release `8.29.10`. Joined a troubleshooting call with Gerardo, Swen, Nico, and Andrew to address Formue’s production issues.

2. **DeutschlandCard:**
   - June 23: Fixed scaling issues by updating the c4 image for DeutschlandCard's database issue ([L3-3151]).
   - June 25: Troubleshooting LDAP networking issues due to their firewall policies requiring NATing external IPs.
   - June 26: Continued work on DeutschlandCard LDAP networking issue.

3. **Customer Investigations:**
   - June 20: Worked on AdidasNeptun ticket ([L3-3148]) related to resizing issues.
   - June 20: Investigated production cost spikes after Juergen raised concerns ([SPOT-25824]).
   - June 23: Resolved reserved capacity issues by identifying and addressing cost inefficiencies. Created a ticket for alerting ([SPOT-25890]).

---

### **Operational Work and Infrastructure Fixes:**  
1. **Ubuntu 24.04 LTS Issues:**
   - June 22: Found that `unattended-upgrades` restarts critical `c4_cloud_command` service. Added necessary configuration to prevent service restarts.

2. **Database Fixes:**
   - June 23, June 26: Deployed fixes for `unattended-upgrades`. Encountered issues with databases going into error states; worked to stabilize them.

3. **Terraform Issues:**
   - June 25: Addressed provider version inconsistencies by updating Terraform workflows, Lambda module provider, and AWS provider.
   - June 26: Fixed issues with Localstack by updating its version.

---

### **PR Reviews and Collaboration:**  
1. Multiple PR reviews and feedback:
   - June 24: Reviewed PRs from Vahid ([vz.SPOT-25385], [#530]) and Haibo ([#527], [#529]).
   - June 25: Reviewed PRs from collaborators ([#533], [#537]).
   - June 26: Assisted Alex with merging DLHC PRs that were previously blocked. Worked with Vahid on Opsgenie webhook ticket creation.

2. Supported colleagues on various tickets and troubleshooting:
   - Helped Leila resolve KMS-related issues.
   - Collaborated with Alex and Vahid to merge DLHC-related PRs.

---

### **Cluster Status and Metrics Collection:**  
1. June 25: Worked on gathering last week's cluster status and created related tickets ([SPOT-25803], [L3-3159]).
2. Created documentation in Jira for these efforts.

---

### **Pending and Follow-Up Tasks:**  
1. DLHC Automation and Testing:
   - Avantika was on vacation; workload shifted while attending to urgent L3 and production issues.

2. Reserved Capacity Optimization:
   - Ticket created to prioritize monitoring and preventing cost spikes due to unused reserved capacity ([SPOT-25890]).

3. Continued troubleshooting for DeutschlandCard's LDAP networking and scaling persistence.

---

### **Challenges and Notes:**  
- High reliance on individual problem-solving due to overlapping priorities between L3 tickets, outages, and infrastructure fixes.
- Time constraints impacted focus on automation tasks like DLHC optimization.

### **Conclusion:**  
The week was marked by a balanced effort addressing critical customer issues, infrastructure stabilization, and collaboration on key development tasks. Notable achievements include resolving reserved capacity cost inefficiencies, infrastructure fixes for Ubuntu, and supporting PR merges to unblock DLHC processes.
```

Command used to update specific release version:

```bash
CONFD_TOKEN=exa_pat_XXXXXXX confd_client saas_db_create_custom_size -a '{"db_name": "SemikronUpdateTest", "platform_name": "aws", "region": "eu-central-1", "size": "XS1", "account_uuid": "org_9aWFmH2ys3gEGhwb", "auto_stop_enabled": True, "auto_stop_idle_time": 120, "version": "@exasol-8.18.2"}'
```

update failed to 8.28.2 the reason is that c4 version doesn't support

since the c4 version is old, we have to update it:

```bash
ccc_wc=$(c4 info @exasol-8.28.2 Dependencies.CCC)
c4 fetch "$ccc_wc"
sudo systemctl stop c4_cloud_command c4
sudo cp ~/.ccc$(c4 info "$ccc_wc" Local-Linkdir)/bin/c4 $(which c4)
sudo systemctl start c4_cloud_command c4 --no-block

```

and then run the command without updating the template:

CCC_UPDATE_TEMPLATE=false c4 update cluster -f -t @exasol-8.28.2 -p <play-id>
```

# October 9, 2024

I talked with Gabriel, and he told me that moving to the new database will include everything, even passwords. So, we can't use the new database's password even if it's shown; we need to stick with the old password.

We ran the command to get the list of SCHEMA TABLES that are corrupted, in this case EXA_STATISTICS with the list of tables.

The next step based on the documentation is to follow and create DDL for a Table. We ran the script create_table_ddl.sql to get the DDL.

and after that we ran the command:

execute script exa_toolbox.create_table_ddl('SOURCE_SCHEMA', 'SOURCE_TABLE', 'TARGET_SCHEMA', 'TARGET_TABLE', true) ;

,0),      "OWNER" VARCHAR(128) UTF8,      "REMARKS" VARCHAR(100000) UTF8,      "START_TIME" TIMESTAMP(3),      "STOP_TIME" TIMESTAMP(3),      "OPERATION_ID" DECIMAL(12,0),      "SUCCESS" CHAR(1) UTF8,

# October 8, 2024

Why Phantom blocks happened when sync is running?

dwad_client setup-param $(dwad_client shortlist) PARAMS "-netmask= -auditing_enabled=1 -defaultSysPasswordHash=91DC4E05D46AD133147119CE1BA2A7C688FF1FBD0DDF63317EAB3DC4 -builtinScriptLanguageName=slc-8.1.0-c4-5-standard-EXASOL-all/ScriptLanguages-standard-EXASOL-all-slc-v8.1.0-CJDGGFPK -lockslb=1 -sandboxPath=/usr/opt/mountjail -cosLogErrors=0 -bucketFSConfigPath=/exa/etc/bucketfs_db.cfg -sysTZ=Europe/Berlin -tlsCertificatePath=/exa/etc/dwad/db_mXzbIA7MRQCrror3yZs4iQ_cert.pem -tlsPrivateKeyPath=/exa/etc/dwad/db_mXzbIA7MRQCrror3yZs4iQ_key.pem -useObjectStorage=1 -objectstorage_volume_id=1 -useCachingVolume=1 -cache_hdd_type=disk2 -cache_volume_size=64373 -snapshot_volume_id=2 -etlJdbcConfigDir=/exa/data/bucketfs/bfsdefault/.dest/default/drivers/jdbc:/exa/data/bucketfs/uploads/.dest/default/drivers/jdbc:/exa/data/bucketfs/bfssaas/.dest/default/drivers/jdbc:/opt/exasol/3rd-party/JDBC/@JDBCVERSION@:/usr/opt/EXASuite-8/3rd-party/JDBC/@JDBCVERSION@:/opt/exasol/db-8.29.1/jdbc -exaop-start-user-args -oidcProviderISS https://identity.exasol.com/ -oidcProviderJKU https://pat-service.cloud.exasol.com/.well-known/jwks.json -oidcProviderTokenEndpoint https://pat-service.cloud.exasol.com/oauth/token -oidcProviderClientId qLrnITv5izAyTxHud2NpFF1oMHpWF0Wq -exaop-stop-user-args -syncBackFormerSnapshots=false"

dwad_client snapshot-restore $(dwad_client shortlist) 4 Rqxxwn-uTomsZackmkialw/id_9889/node_3/snapshot_backup_202410081100

How can we tell if the data is up-to-date?

To check data integrity in the database, use this command:

```
checkdata -pers_volume_id 0 -mode data_integrity
```

To make sure all checksums are correct, use:

```
checkdata -pers_volume_id 0 -mode checksum
```

We found corrupted data in the old database after running these checks and looking at the logs, there are a large number of data blocks that are corrupt.

Questions:

1. Why is the old database still working without any connection issues, even though there's corruption?
2. How can we check if any data is missing?

Why Phantom blocks happened in new database?
Is it because of a sync between the old and new database, is corrupting data on the way?
```

# June 14, 2024

09:42 AM

Checking the costs history for a ticket : https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-20928

10:41 AM

Creating a list of onboarding new members. [onboarding-newmember](daily_notes/how-to/onboarding-newmember.md)

11:14 AM

Test 8.28.2 release in staging database with new c4 update cluster command.

Semirkon c4 version: 0.4.10
it is not possible to follow with the new c4 update cluster command, as it does not support it.

Env: staging
DBUUID: k0I5h3ITQFae8UZcxUokiQ
region: eu-central-1

Command used to update specific release version:

```bash
CONFD_TOKEN=exa_pat_XXXXXXX confd_client saas_db_create_custom_size -a '{"db_name": "SemikronUpdateTest", "platform_name": "aws", "region": "eu-central-1", "size": "XS1", "account_uuid": "org_9aWFmH2ys3gEGhwb", "auto_stop_enabled": True, "auto_stop_idle_time": 120, "version": "@exasol-8.18.2"}'
```

update failed to 8.28.2 the reason is that c4 version doesn't support

since the c4 version is old, we have to update it:

```bash
ccc_wc=$(c4 info @exasol-8.28.2 Dependencies.CCC)
c4 fetch "$ccc_wc"
sudo systemctl stop c4_cloud_command c4
sudo cp ~/.ccc$(c4 info "$ccc_wc" Local-Linkdir)/bin/c4 $(which c4)
sudo systemctl start c4_cloud_command c4 --no-block

```

and then run the command without updating the template:

CCC_UPDATE_TEMPLATE=false c4 update cluster -f -t @exasol-8.28.2 -p <play-id>
```

## Week_26.md
```
**Weekly Report: June 19, 2025 - June 26, 2025**

### Summary of Activities:

This week involved multiple efforts across L3 tickets, troubleshooting customer issues, cost optimization initiatives, PR reviews, and infrastructure fixes. Here's a breakdown of key actions and accomplishments:

---

### **Customer Support and Troubleshooting:**  
1. **Formue Issues:**
   - June 19: Attempted fixes for persistent production issues.
   - June 20: Resolved production database outage after release `8.29.10`. Joined a troubleshooting call with Gerardo, Swen, Nico, and Andrew to address Formue’s production issues.

2. **DeutschlandCard:**
   - June 23: Fixed scaling issues by updating the c4 image for DeutschlandCard's database issue ([L3-3151]).
   - June 25: Troubleshooting LDAP networking issues due to their firewall policies requiring NATing external IPs.
   - June 26: Continued work on DeutschlandCard LDAP networking issue.

3. **Customer Investigations:**
   - June 20: Worked on AdidasNeptun ticket ([L3-3148]) related to resizing issues.
   - June 20: Investigated production cost spikes after Juergen raised concerns ([SPOT-25824]).
   - June 23: Resolved reserved capacity issues by identifying and addressing cost inefficiencies. Created a ticket for alerting ([SPOT-25890]).

---

### **Operational Work and Infrastructure Fixes:**  
1. **Ubuntu 24.04 LTS Issues:**
   - June 22: Found that `unattended-upgrades` restarts critical `c4_cloud_command` service. Added necessary configuration to prevent service restarts.

2. **Database Fixes:**
   - June 23, June 26: Deployed fixes for `unattended-upgrades`. Encountered issues with databases going into error states; worked to stabilize them.

3. **Terraform Issues:**
   - June 25: Addressed provider version inconsistencies by updating Terraform workflows, Lambda module provider, and AWS provider.
   - June 26: Fixed issues with Localstack by updating its version.

---

### **PR Reviews and Collaboration:**  
1. Multiple PR reviews and feedback:
   - June 24: Reviewed PRs from Vahid ([vz.SPOT-25385], [#530]) and Haibo ([#527], [#529]).
   - June 25: Reviewed PRs from collaborators ([#533], [#537]).
   - June 26: Assisted Alex with merging DLHC PRs that were previously blocked. Worked with Vahid on Opsgenie webhook ticket creation.

2. Supported colleagues on various tickets and troubleshooting:
   - Helped Leila resolve KMS-related issues.
   - Collaborated with Alex and Vahid to merge DLHC-related PRs.

---

### **Cluster Status and Metrics Collection:**  
1. June 25: Worked on gathering last week's cluster status and created related tickets ([SPOT-25803], [L3-3159]).
2. Created documentation in Jira for these efforts.

---

### **Pending and Follow-Up Tasks:**  
1. DLHC Automation and Testing:
   - Avantika was on vacation; workload shifted while attending to urgent L3 and production issues.

2. Reserved Capacity Optimization:
   - Ticket created to prioritize monitoring and preventing cost spikes due to unused reserved capacity ([SPOT-25890]).

3. Continued troubleshooting for DeutschlandCard's LDAP networking and scaling persistence.

---

### **Challenges and Notes:**  
- High reliance on individual problem-solving due to overlapping priorities between L3 tickets, outages, and infrastructure fixes.
- Time constraints impacted focus on automation tasks like DLHC optimization.

### **Conclusion:**  
The week was marked by a balanced effort addressing critical customer issues, infrastructure stabilization, and collaboration on key development tasks. Notable achievements include resolving reserved capacity cost inefficiencies, infrastructure fixes for Ubuntu, and supporting PR merges to unblock DLHC processes.
```

On call with [[Mihai]] [[Andrew]] [[Fabian]] about users that got hacked and we had to check whether these users are still in the SaaS.

```sql
SELECT
    u."ID",
    u."UUID",
    u."FIRSTNAME",
    u."LASTNAME",
    u."EMAIL",
    u."ACCOUNT_UUID",
    u."STATUS",
    u."CREATED_AT",
    u."IS_BLOCKED",
    a."USER_UUID",
    a."AUTH0_USER_ID",
    a."CREATED_AT" AS auth0_created_at
FROM
    "USERS" u
LEFT JOIN
    "AUTH0_USERS" a
    ON u."UUID" = a."USER_UUID"
LEFT JOIN
    "AUTH0_ORGS" o
    ON u."ACCOUNT_UUID" = o."ACCOUNT_UUID"
WHERE
    u."EMAIL" IN ('misiuwmfolo@tiscali.it',
'shoxha@searchlightcap.com',
'thomasmeyerpay@mail.ru',
'tigipa@thichanthit.com');

```
```

# March 17, 2025


Reviewed [[Haibo]] ticket for testing database with 1 node and 4 nodes https://exasol.atlassian.net/browse/SPOT-24210

Reviewing [[Avantika]] PR for removing DBConfigure function and using StepFunction instead. SPOT-24272

Reviewing the suggestion for my PR's

We have [[Adena]] update today to a version : @saas-8.29.8


Me and [[Avantika]] while updating [[Adena]] we found out that Lifecycle Rule for S3 bucket is different from November until Today. 

The code change that I introduced is not anymore enabled from November.


Before November the code was correct but somehow changed.

Code Snippet from c4: ╭─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╮
│ src/modules/saas/aws-sdk-v1/aws.go                                  │
│─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────│
│ config_s3 := &s3.BucketLifecycleConfiguration{                      │
│   Rules: []*s3.LifecycleRule{                                       │
│     {                                                               │
│       ID:     aws.String("Delete expired backups"),                 │
│       Filter: &s3.LifecycleRuleFilter{Prefix: aws.String("")},      │
│       Status: aws.String("Enabled"),                                │
│       NoncurrentVersionExpiration: &s3.NoncurrentVersionExpiration{ │
│         NoncurrentDays: aws.Int64(7),                               │
│       },                                                            │
│       Expiration: &s3.LifecycleExpiration{                          │
│         ExpiredObjectDeleteMarker: aws.Bool(true),                  │
│       },                                                            │
│     },                                                              │
│   },                                                                │
│ }                                                                   │
- [ ] ╰─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯
```

## DeutschlandCard-meeting.md
```
---
id: DeutschlandCard-meeting
aliases: []
tags:
  - DeutschlandCard
---

### **Step-by-Step Guide: AWS Direct Connect Setup**

#### **1. Plan Your Setup**
   - **Determine Your Needs**: Decide what you need the Direct Connect connection for (e.g., connecting AWS to Oracle Cloud (OCI) through FastConnect).
   - **Location**: Identify the AWS Direct Connect **location** (data center) that is geographically closest to your hardware or OCI region.
   - **Connection Speed**: Choose a connection capacity (e.g., 1 Gbps or 10 Gbps).
   - **Existing vs New Setup**:
     - If you already have an existing Direct Connect, you can reuse it, as mentioned.
     - If you're starting fresh, you'll need to provision a new Direct Connect.

---

#### **2. Request AWS Direct Connect**
   - Log in to your **AWS Management Console**.
   - Go to the **Direct Connect dashboard**.
   - Select "Create a New Connection" and complete the following details:
     - **Location**: Choose an AWS Direct Connect location.
     - **Port speed**: Specify your desired capacity (e.g., 1 Gbps or 10 Gbps).
     - **Connection Type**: Options include a dedicated network connection (physical) or a hosted connection (via an AWS Direct Connect Partner).
   - After submitting the request, AWS Direct Connect will allocate a port or virtual interface (VIF).

---

#### **3. Order a Physical Cross-Connect**
   - Work with either:
     - **AWS Direct Connect Partners** or your colocation provider to set up the physical cross-connect to the AWS Direct Connect router at the selected location/data center.
   - AWS provides a Letter of Authority (LOA)–Cable Diagram after approval, which is required to complete the setup of the connection at the colocation facility.

---

#### **4. Configure the Virtual Interface (VIF)**
   - After the physical cross-connect is established, log in to the **AWS Management Console** to create a **Virtual Interface (VIF)**.
   - There are two types of VIFs to choose from:
     1. **Private VIF**: For securely accessing private resources like VPCs.
     2. **Public VIF**: For accessing AWS public services (e.g., S3, DynamoDB).
   - For OCI integration, you’ll likely configure a **Private VIF** to connect your VPC to the Oracle Cloud.

---

#### **5. Set Up AWS Side: Configure VPC Settings**
   - Link the Direct Connect to your AWS **Virtual Private Gateway (VGW)** or **Transit Gateway**:
     - Create a **Virtual Private Gateway** in AWS if you don’t already have one.
     - Attach this gateway to the VPC hosting your EXASOL SaaS service.
   - This facilitates the connection between the Direct Connect VIF and your resources.

---

#### **6. Set Up Oracle Cloud Side: Configure OCI FastConnect**
   - Work within your Oracle Cloud Infrastructure (OCI) console:
     - Use OCI **FastConnect** to establish a connection between your Oracle networks and AWS Direct Connect.
     - Define routing policies between your Oracle Virtual Cloud Network (VCN) and AWS Virtual Private Gateway.
   - Provision a **FastConnect Virtual Circuit**, ensuring it aligns with the AWS Direct Connect setup.

---

#### **7. Configure BGP Peering**
   - Direct Connect uses **Border Gateway Protocol (BGP)** for routing. Configure BGP both on:
     - AWS side: Specify your ASN (Autonomous System Number) and other BGP settings.
     - OCI side: Set up BGP peering with the corresponding route information.
   - Exchange routes between your AWS VPC and OCI VCN.

---

#### **8. Test Connectivity**
   - Verify that traffic flows between your AWS VPC hosting EXASOL and your Oracle Cloud account through the VPN tunnel and Direct Connect.
   - Test for latency, throughput, and security expectations.

---

#### **9. Monitor and Adjust**
   - Use AWS tools such as **CloudWatch** for monitoring the connection.
   - If needed, adjust the speed of Direct Connect monthly (1 Gbps to higher speeds).
   - Ensure no other database access occurs outside of the VPN tunnel, as EXASOL would handle this restriction.

---

### Costs and Customer Responsibility
- The customer is responsible for all AWS Direct Connect and OCI FastConnect costs. This includes colocation charges, connection speed adjustments, and any outbound traffic costs.

---

```

---

### **Weekly Notes: April 24 – April 30, 2025**

#### **Key Topics**
- Collaboration and discussions around AWS EventBridge Rules and StepFunction usage.
- Maintenance and release updates for the SaaS analytics pipeline and monitoring of system SLI.
- Significant teamwork involving [[Avantika]], [[Mihai]], and [[Haibo]] to address pipeline dispatching challenges.

---

#### **Highlights from Daily Notes**

### **April 25, 2025**
- Continued work on implementing an EventBridge rule in the CT account targeting the production account to trigger the SaaS analytics pipeline.
- Updated SLI metrics and discussed strategies for monitoring thresholds during maintenance windows.
- Call with [[Mihai]] to explore StepFunction architecture enhancements.

---

### **April 28, 2025**
- Maintenance window in production concluded successfully. SaaS release version: `20250417`.
- Conducted post-release analysis to ensure full functionality of SaaS analytics workflows were restored and optimized.
- Troubleshooting minor discrepancies in StepFunction state transitions during dispatch processes with [[Haibo]].

---

### **April 29, 2025**
- Focus shifted to debugging cross-account routing errors in EventBridge configurations.
- Collaborated with [[Avantika]] and [[Mihai]] to refine the integration testing framework for better validation of inter-account triggers.
- Finalized a checklist for system-wide monitoring using enhanced SLI metrics and StepFunction introspection.

---

### **April 30, 2025**
- Wrapped up week-long efforts on the EventBridge-Production pipeline. Integration is now streamlined.
- Retroactive review of maintenance events highlighted significant improvements in system telemetry.
- Brief meeting with [[Avantika]] and [[Haibo]] to document best practices for managing inter-service dispatch mechanisms.

---

#### **Action Items for Next Week**
1. **EventBridge Rules**: Establish protocols for automated retries in case of cross-account errors.
2. **SLI Metrics**: Address edge-case monitoring gaps identified during post-maintenance review.
3. **StepFunction Optimization**: Review execution visualization and document bottleneck mitigation processes.

---

- [[Avantika]]: Played a critical role in EventBridge setup and integration testing.
- [[Mihai]]: Contributed valuable insights to enhance StepFunction architecture and execution flow.
- [[Haibo]]: Assisted in fine-tuning dispatch error resolution processes.

## 2025-06-22.md
```
---
id: "2025-06-22"
aliases:
  - June 22, 2025
tags:
  - daily-notes
---

# June 22, 2025

Found out that unattended-upgrades restarts c4_cloud_command on new Ubuntu 24.04 LTS. We had to add a conf to not restart the service.

```
/etc/needrestart/conf.d/exasol.conf

# Do not automatically restart c4_cloud_command
# because that's the service which runs the whole
# Exasol stack of services, including databases.
$nrconf{override_rc}{qr(^c4_cloud_command\.service$)} = 0;

```
```
